CREATE TABLE users (
	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	role VARCHAR(50), 
	is_email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	subscription_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, 
	created_at DATETIME NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id)
)
